Compute gradient for log-sum-exp all p: weights w_p = exp(β(c_p - c_max)) / sum. Often many linear? If β small, all weights. Compute gradient via convolutions:
For each sign, ℓ, weight w_ℓ^+ and w_ℓ^- for d=+ℓ and d=-ℓ.
Positive slopes contribution:
g[j] += w_pos[ℓ]*( 1_{j≥ℓ}(1-h[j-ℓ]) - 1_{j≤n-1-ℓ} h[j+ℓ] )
Can rewrite:
Term A_j = Σ_{ℓ=0}^j w_pos[ℓ] (for positive first)
Term B_j = Σ_{ℓ=0}^j w_pos[ℓ] h[j-ℓ] = (w_pos * h)[j] truncated with w index ℓ ≤j.
Term C_j = Σ_{ℓ=0}^{n-1-j} w_pos[ℓ] h[j+ℓ] = (w_pos * h)[j] right? It is cross-correlation. Together A - B - C.
Negative contribution:
g[j] += w_neg[ℓ]*( 1_{j≤n-1-ℓ}(1-h[j+ℓ]) - 1_{j≥ℓ} h[j-ℓ] ) with ℓ=1..n-1 (w_neg[0]=0).
Term D_j = -Σ_{ℓ=0}^{n-1-j} w_neg[ℓ] h[j+ℓ]
Term E_j = -Σ_{ℓ=1}^j w_neg[ℓ] h[j-ℓ]
Term F_j = +Σ_{ℓ=1}^{n-1-j} w_neg[ℓ] (for first). Note d=0 can be in pos with ℓ=0; negative ℓ=0 none.
Let's derive more systematically for each j:
g0 = dx * [ Σ_{ℓ=0}^{n-1} w_plus[ℓ] 1_{j≥ℓ? and j? first cond j>=ℓ? for plus first requires j>=ℓ and j<=n-1? also second plus first i=j-ℓ valid if 0≤j-ℓ≤n-1-ℓ => j≥ℓ and j≤n-1. Always if j≥ℓ. But c_plus[ℓ] only defined ℓ=0..n-1. So first plus: ℓ≤j -> weight w_plus[ℓ]*(1 - h[j-ℓ]).
Plus second: ℓ≤n-1-j -> - w_plus[ℓ] h[j+ℓ].
For negative ℓ=1..n-1:
Neg first: requires j≤n-1-ℓ -> w_neg[ℓ]*(1 - h[j+ℓ]).
Neg second: j≥ℓ -> -w_neg[ℓ] h[j-ℓ].

def coord_cd(h,n, max_passes, deadline, k_max=180, margin_raw=3.0):
    h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    for p in range(max_passes):
        if time>deadline: break
        c = np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')  # raw
        maxc = c.max()
        improved=False
        for idx in range(n):
            h0=h[idx]
            if h0<=1e-12 or h0>=1-1e-12: continue
            # affected full indices
            m_f_lo=max(0,n-1-idx); m_f_hi=min(2*n-2,2*n-2-idx)
            m_b_lo=max(0,idx); m_b_hi=min(2*n-2,idx+n-1)
            m_lo=min(m_f_lo,m_b_lo); m_hi=max(m_f_hi,m_b_hi)
            # initial unaff = c outside [m_lo,m_hi]
            unaff = 0
            if m_lo>0: unaff=max(unaff,c[:m_lo].max())
            if m_hi<2*n-2: unaff=max(unaff,c[m_hi+1:].max())
            # candidate ms: affected indices with c >= maxc - margin; if count>k_max take top by c
            vals=c[m_lo:m_hi+1]
            thresh = maxc - margin_raw
            sel = np.nonzero(vals >= thresh)[0]
            if len(sel)>k_max:
                # take top k_max by val among affected
                # include center globally
                part = np.argpartition(vals, -k_max)[-k_max:]
                sel = np.unique(np.concatenate((sel? part, [center-r?])))
                sel = sel[sel>=0]
            else: sel = sel
            # ensure center n-1 in sel
            if n-1 not in sel: sel = np.append(sel,n-1)
            ms = m_lo + sel
            # unaff should include affected not selected as constant? They are actually affected. To be conservative, set unaff = max(unaff, max c[skipped]) but skipped may increase. We can't exact. If skipped within margin, their possible increase ≤2, so we can add penalty? We can treat as lower bound: c_skipped(current)+2? For minimization, using too low unaff may choose invalid x where skipped > value. To be safe, after candidate x, we could verify full c max? That's O(n) per candidate, too heavy. Could include skipped in unaff as c_skipped + 2 (upper bound) maybe too conservative but safe? Since |slope|≤2 and |x-h0|≤max(h0,1-h0)≤1, skipped line value ≤ current + 2*max_delta_hi? More exactly max increase ≤ 2*min(max(h0,1-h0)?). For skipped, when x changes in [h0,1] or [0,h0], increase ≤ |a|*delta ≤2*range. Add to current c_skipped upper bound. If we add to unaff, solver may be conservative but avoids invalid. We can add `unaff = max(unaff, max_skipped + 2*max_delta)` where max_delta=max(h0,1-h0). But this may overestimate and stop improvements. Use smaller margin? Since if skipped below maxc-margin, even +2 may be below current target. Fine.
```

If k >= 0:
  slope = 0
  if idx - k >=0: slope += 1 - h[idx-k]
  if idx + k < n: slope -= h[idx+k]
If k <0:
  ℓ=-k
  if idx + ℓ < n: slope += 1 - h[idx+ℓ]
  if idx - ℓ >=0: slope -= h[idx-ℓ]
Center k=0: both conditions true: slope = 1-h0 - h0 =1-2h0, plus quadratic.
This matches positive formula and negative formula.

  second: idx+ks<n -> ks<=n-1-idx: slope -= h[idx+ks]
For neg:
  ell=-k[neg]
  first: ell<=n-1-idx: slope += 1 - h[idx+ell]

def f_val(x, slopes, intercepts, center_val_func, unaff):
    y = intercepts + x*slopes
    v = y.max() if len else 0
    if center: v=max(v, center_const + x - x*x)

def max_affine_x(slopes,intercepts,lo,hi,h0):
   # returns candidate x and affval
   if len==0: return h0
   # if all slopes equal? handle
   order = np.argsort(slopes, kind='mergesort')
   m = slopes[order]; b = intercepts[order]
   # combine same slopes: keep max b
   hull_m=[]; hull_b=[]; hull_x=[]
   for i in range(len(m)):
      if hull_m and m[i]==hull_m[-1]:
         if b[i]<=hull_b[-1]+1e-15: continue
         else: pop
      if not hull_m: hull_m.append(m[i]); hull_b.append(b[i]); hull_x.append(-np.inf)
      else:
         # x where new >= last
         denom=m[i]-hull_m[-1]
         if abs(denom)<1e-15: ...
         x=(hull_b[-1]-b[i])/denom
         while hull_m and x <= hull_x[-1]+1e-12:
             hull_m.pop(); hull_b.pop(); hull_x.pop()
             if not: break
             denom=m[i]-hull_m[-1]; x=(hull_b[-1]-b[i])/denom
         if not hull_m:
            hull_x.append(-np.inf)
         else:
            hull_x.append(x)
   # Evaluate candidates:
   xs = [lo,hi,h0]
   # For each transition i from i to i+1, x_start = hull_x[i+1]
   for i in range(len(hull_x)-1):
       xm = (hull_x[i]+hull_x[i+1])/2? But we need slope crossing.

Now `minimize_axis1d(slopes, intercepts, c_center, h0, unaff)`:
- slopes, intercepts arrays for noncenter lines length L-1. Could be empty? M=0? n=1? Not relevant (n must even? target). If no lines, H=Z concave, min at bounds (0,1) (since concave) or if constant? If unaff maybe max. We'll handle.
- Build hull of lines. For max envelope, slopes can be any. But lines are exact; if no lines, A=-inf.
Hull building with arrays length up to 1200. Use `order=np.argsort(slopes, kind='mergesort')`; then combine equal slopes (tol? Floating slopes from sums of h, could have duplicates but not exact. Use exact equality? Floating may differ by 1e-17. For hull, no need exact grouping; if denom tiny, handle. Use `eps=1e-15`. To keep robust, if abs(denom)<eps, keep larger intercept and skip.
Pseudo:
```
def build_hull(slopes, intercepts):
    order=np.argsort(slopes)
    ms=slopes[order]; bs=intercepts[order]
    hm=[]; hb=[]; hx=[]
    for m,b in zip(ms,bs):
        if len(hm)>0:
            if abs(m-hm[-1]) < 1e-14:
                if b <= hb[-1] + 1e-15: continue
                else: hm.pop(); hb.pop(); hx.pop()
        if len(hm)==0:
            hm.append(m); hb.append(b); hx.append(-np.inf)
        else:
            x = (hb[-1]-b)/(m-hm[-1])
            while len(hm)>0 and x <= hx[-1] + 1e-12:
                hm.pop(); hb.pop(); hx.pop()
                if not hm: break
                x = (hb[-1]-b)/(m-hm[-1])
            if not hm:
                hm.append(m); hb.append(b); hx.append(-np.inf)
            else:
                hm.append(m); hb.append(b); hx.append(x)
    return hm,hb,hx
```
Need when slope decreasing? We sort ascending, for max envelope, new line with higher slope becomes better to right. If b is lower, intersection maybe -inf; if x <= previous start, pop.

   - For each interval, A can attain local min at start if m>0, end if m<0, any if m≈0. But global min of convex A can be found by subgradient: if first interval slope >0 -> 0; if last slope <0 ->1; else find transition where m_i<0 and m_{i+1}>0 -> breakpoint hx[i+1]. Simpler compute after hull: clamp hx breakpoints. But due intervals with zero slope, any. We'll implement using scanning.
   - D_min_i: parabola x0=(1-m)/2 (since derivative 2x+m-1=0). clip to [start,end]. D_val=(x0*x0 + (m-1)*x0 + b) (unless center absent). Track global minD and x_Dmin.
   - Also collect candidate x values: start,end, x_A (from transition), root estimates.

Let's implement slopes vectorized carefully with local variables for speed.
```
def affected_slopes(h, idx, n):
    M = idx if idx > n-1-idx else n-1-idx
    L = 2*M+1
    k = np.arange(-M, M+1)
    slopes = np.zeros(L)
    pos = k >= 0
    # Use indices where pos and k values
    jpos = np.nonzero(pos)[0]
    ks = k[jpos]
    m1 = ks <= idx
    if np.any(m1):
        sel = jpos[m1]
        kk = ks[m1]
        slopes[sel] += 1.0 - h[idx - kk]
    m2 = ks <= n-1-idx
    if np.any(m2):
        sel = jpos[m2]
        kk = ks[m2]
        slopes[sel] -= h[idx + kk]
    jneg = np.nonzero(~pos)[0]
    ell = -k[jneg]
    m1n = ell <= n-1-idx
    if np.any(m1n):
        sel = jneg[m1n]; ee=ell[m1n]; slopes[sel] += 1.0 - h[idx + ee]
    m2n = ell <= idx
    if np.any(m2n):
        sel = jneg[m2n]; ee=ell[m2n]; slopes[sel] -= h[idx - ee]
    return p_lo, p_hi, slopes
```

Now, let's design `project_feasible` exact:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    target=0.5*n
    # water fill lambda
    lo = -float(np.max(h)) - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(h)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.sum(np.clip(h+mid,0,1))
       if s > target: hi=mid
       else: lo=mid
    h=np.clip(h+ (lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    diff=target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       interior = np.where((h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12))[0]
       if len(interior)>0:
          # adjust one with enough room
          for i in interior:
             add = diff
             if 0 <= h[i]+add <=1:
               h[i]+=add; break
          # if not, distribute among interior
          if abs(target - h.sum())>1e-12:
             h[interior] += diff/len(interior)
             h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       else:
          # scale all? if all saturated impossible target within [0,n]
          h = np.clip(h + diff/n,0,1)
    # final tiny clip and adjust
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>0:
       interior = np.where((h>0)&(h<1))[0]
       if len(interior)>0:
          h[interior[0]] += diff
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
Since target may be non-integer for odd n but n likely even. Use `target = n*0.5`.

`compute_c5`: use `np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full'))* (2.0/n)`. For raw max use `compute_raw_c` maybe.

def coord_cd(h,n,max_passes,deadline,k_cap=1200,use_all=True):
    h = h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    center=n-1
    for p in range(max_passes):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       c_full = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
       hs = h.copy()
       h_new = h.copy()
       max_rel = 1e-13
       for idx in range(n):
          h0 = hs[idx]
          if h0 <= 1e-13 or h0 >= 1.0-1e-13: continue
          M = idx if idx > n-1-idx else n-1-idx
          p_lo = center - M; p_hi = center + M
          L = 2*M+1
          c_seg = c_full[p_lo:p_hi+1]
          unaff = 0.0
          if p_lo>0: unaff = max(unaff, float(c_full[:p_lo].max()))
          if p_hi<2*n-2: unaff = max(unaff, float(c_full[p_hi+1:].max()))
          # build slopes
          slopes = np.empty(L)
          # loop q/d
          for q in range(L):
             d = -M + q
             a=0.0
             if d >= 0:
                if idx - d >= 0: a += 1.0 - hs[idx-d]
                if idx + d < n: a -= hs[idx+d]
             else:
                e = -d
                if idx + e < n: a += 1.0 - hs[idx+e]
                if idx - e >= 0: a -= hs[idx-e]
             slopes[q]=a
          # find center rel = M
          center_rel = M
          # If L > k_cap, select lines? For exact, use all. We'll use all for n up to maybe 800? L up to 1599. Time maybe. Set k_cap large 2000 to use all. But if n=1000, L 1999 okay.
          if L > k_cap: # approximate select top
             # choose top by c_seg + maybe include center
             ...
          else:
             ms_rel = np.arange(L)
```

```
def lse_optimize(h,n,max_iter,alphas,deadline):
  h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5
  dx=2.0/n
  for alpha in alphas:
    if time>deadline: break
    # maybe recompute direction each iter
    step_multipliers = [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,0.02]
    for it in range(max_iter):
      if time>deadline: break
      c = np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')
      C = c.max()*dx
      # weights
      if alpha small, z = alpha*(c-c.max())
      w = np.exp(z)
      wsum = w.sum()
      if wsum <=0: break
      w /= wsum
      w_plus = w[n-1:2*n-1]
      w_neg = np.zeros(n); w_neg[1:] = w[n-2::-1]
      K = w_plus + w_neg; K[0] = 2*w_plus[0]
      Kp = np.zeros(2*n-1)
      Kp[n-1]=K[0]
      if n>1:
        Kp[0:n-1] = K[1:][::-1]
        Kp[n:] = K[1:]
      nfft=1; need=3*n-2; while nfft<need: nfft*=2
      total_h = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(h,nfft)*np.fft.rfft(Kp,nfft), nfft)[n-1:n-1+n]
      cum_plus = np.cumsum(w_plus)
      neg_sum = w_neg.sum()
      prefix_neg = np.cumsum(w_neg)
      tail_neg = neg_sum - prefix_neg
      grad = dx*(cum_plus + tail_neg - total_h)
      grad -= grad.mean()
      gnorm = float(np.sqrt(np.dot(grad,grad)))
      if gnorm < 1e-14: break
      base = 0.2 / gnorm
      accepted=False
      # Try step sizes from small to large? Use [0.1,0.2,0.5,1,2,0.05]
      for mult in (0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,0.05,4.0):
         h2 = project_feasible(h - base*mult*grad, n)
         C2 = compute_c5(h2,n)
         if C2 < best_c - 1e-12:
             h=h2; best_c=C2; accepted=True; break
         if C2 < C - 1e-13:
             # even if not global best? accept if low? Use best_c
             pass
      if not accepted:
         # try reverse small? if gradient ascent? no.
         break
  return h,best_c
```
Concern: `project_feasible` is expensive (water-fill 60 iterations O(n)) per step. 7 steps * many iterations * multiple alphas could be heavy. n=600, project 60*600 C ~ small but Python loop. ```
def project_feasible_fast(x,n):
  x=np.clip(x,0,1)
  diff=0.5*n - x.sum()
  if abs(diff)<1e-13: return x
  # iterative distribute to unsaturated few times
  for _ in range(20):
    if diff>0:
      room=1-x; tot=room.sum(); if tot<=0: break; x += diff/tot*room
    else:
      tot=x.sum(); if tot<=0: break; x += diff/tot*x
    x=np.clip(x,0,1)
    if abs(0.5*n-x.sum())<1e-13: break
  return adjust exact
```

from bisect import bisect_right

def active_value(x, hm, hb, hx):
    if not hm:
        return -1e300
    i = bisect_right(hx, x) - 1
    if i < 0: i = 0
    elif i >= len(hm): i = len(hm)-1
    return hm[i]*x + hb[i]
```

`build_hull(m_arr,b_arr)`:
```
def build_hull(m, b):
    order = np.argsort(m, kind='mergesort')
    ms = m[order]; bs = b[order]
    hm=[]; hb=[]; hx=[]
    eps=1e-14
    for ii in range(len(ms)):
        mv=float(ms[ii]); bv=float(bs[ii])
        if len(hm) and abs(mv - hm[-1]) < eps:
            if bv <= hb[-1] + 1e-15:
                continue
            hm.pop(); hb.pop(); hx.pop()
        if not len(hm):
            hm.append(mv); hb.append(bv); hx.append(-np.inf)
        else:
            # if same slope? pop if necessary handled
            denom = mv - hm[-1]
            if abs(denom) < eps:
                # if new line better already popped old if needed; skip
                if bv > hb[-1]:
                   hb[-1]=bv
                continue
            x = (hb[-1] - bv) / denom
            while len(hm) and x <= hx[-1] + 1e-12:
                hm.pop(); hb.pop(); hx.pop()
                if not len(hm):
                    break
                denom = mv - hm[-1]
                if abs(denom) < eps:
                    if bv > hb[-1]:
                       hb[-1]=bv
                       x = hx[-1] - 1.0  # force keep? Hmm
                    else:
                       x = hx[-1] + 1.0
                else:
                    x = (hb[-1] - bv) / denom
            if not len(hm):
                hm.append(mv); hb.append(bv); hx.append(-np.inf)
            else:
                hm.append(mv); hb.append(bv); hx.append(x)
    return hm,hb,hx
```
Need robust when x -inf? Intersections can be large. Fine.

Could duplicate slopes with nearly same; if new improves, pop old and recompute with previous. The same slope handling in while may be tricky. Since slopes from sums of h in [-1,1], not many exact duplicates but possible. `affine_min_x(hm, hx, h0)`:
```
def affine_min_x(hm,hx,h0):
  if not hm: return 0.5
  eps=1e-12
  if hm[0] > eps: return 0.0
  if hm[-1] < -eps: return 1.0
  for i in range(len(hm)-1):
    if hm[i] <= eps and hm[i+1] >= -eps:
      if abs(hm[i]) <= eps and abs(hm[i+1]) <= eps? Actually slope zero interval, choose h0 if within interval.
      if abs(hm[i]) <= eps:
         a = 0.0 if i==0 else hx[i]
         b = 1.0 if i+1==len(hx) else hx[i+1]
         a=max(0.0,a); b=min(1.0,b)
         if b<a: return 0.5
         if a <= h0 <= b: return h0
         return 0.5*(a+b)
      # crossing
      x = hx[i+1] if i+1 < len(hx) else 1.0
      if x < 0: return 0.0
      if x > 1: return 1.0
      return float(x)
  # no crossing? first slope <=0 and last >=0? maybe zero last
  if hm[-1] <= eps: return 1.0
  return 0.0
```
Need hx list length = len(hm), hx[i+1] breakpoint where line i+1 starts. For i crossing, x=hx[i+1]. If hx is inf, clamp.

def minimize_h_axis(slopes, intercepts, center_c, h0, unaff):
    if slopes.size:
       hm,hb,hx=build_hull(slopes,intercepts)
    else: hm=[]...
    Zc = None if center_c is None else float(center_c) - float(h0)*(1.0-float(h0))
    x_A = affine_min_x(hm,hx,h0) if hm else 0.5
    candidates=[0.0,1.0,float(h0),x_A]
    if Zc is not None:
       # scan D min
       bestD=inf; xD=0.5
       for i in range(len(hm)):
          a = -np.inf if i==0 else hx[i]
          b = np.inf if i+1==len(hx) else hx[i+1]
          if a < 0.0: a=0.0
          if b > 1.0: b=1.0
          if b <= a: continue
          m=hm[i]; bb=hb[i]
          x0=(1.0-m)*0.5
          if x0 < a: xi=a
          elif x0 > b: xi=b
          else: xi=x0
          D = xi*xi + (m-1.0)*xi + (bb - Zc)
          # also endpoints? x0 clipped covers min; if interval and xi outside endpoint. But if D min at endpoint, xi endpoint.
          if D < bestD:
             bestD=D; xD=xi
       if not candidates: candidates.append(xD)
       candidates.append(xD)
       if bestD < -1e-10 and hm:
          def Dval? cannot closure. Need top-level function d_eval(x,hm,hb,hx,Zc)
          left = 0.0
          if d_eval(0.0,...) >= 0:
              a=0.0; b=xD
              # ensure D(b)<0; if not, due rounding skip
              for _ in range(45): mid=(a+b)/2; if d_eval(mid)<0: b=mid else a=mid
              left=(a+b)/2
          else:
              left=0.0
          if d_eval(1.0,...) >=0:
              a=xD; b=1.0
              for ... if d_eval(mid)<0: a=mid else b=mid
              right=(a+b)/2
          else: right=1.0
          candidates += [left,right]
    # eval candidates
    best_x=float(h0); best_val=1e300
    for x in candidates:
      if x<0: x=0; if x>1: x=1
      Av = active_value(x,hm,hb,hx)
      Hv = Av
      if Zc is not None:
         Zv = Zc + x - x*x
         if Zv > Hv: Hv=Zv
      if unaff > Hv: Hv=unaff
      if Hv < best_val:
         best_val=Hv; best_x=x
    return best_x, best_val
```

Need top-level `d_eval`:
```
def d_eval(x, hm, hb, hx, Zc):
    x=float(x)
    i=bisect_right(hx, x)-1
    if i<0:i=0
    elif i>=len(hm):i=len(hm)-1
    A=hm[i]*x+hb[i]
    return A - (Zc + x - x*x)
```

Now coordinate pass in `coord_cd`:
```
def coord_cd(h,n,max_passes,deadline,k_all=1200):
    h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    center=n-1
    no_improve=0
    for p in range(max_passes):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       c_full=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
       current_max=float(c_full.max())
       hs=h
       h_new=h.copy()
       improved=False
       for idx in range(n):
          if (idx & 15)==0 and time.time() > deadline: break
          h0=hs[idx]
          if h0 <= 1e-12 or h0 >=1-1e-12: continue
          M = idx if idx > n-1-idx else n-1-idx
          p_lo=center-M; L=2*M+1
          c_seg=c_full[p_lo:p_lo+L]
          unaff=0.0
          if p_lo>0: unaff=max(unaff, float(c_full[:p_lo].max()))
          p_hi=p_lo+L-1
          if p_hi < 2*n-2: unaff=max(unaff,float(c_full[p_hi+1:].max()))
          # select rel
          if L <= k_all:
             rels=np.arange(L)
          else:
             kmax=min(600, k_all//2?)
             thresh=float(c_seg.max()) - 1.5
             sel=np.flatnonzero(c_seg >= thresh)
             if len(sel) > kmax:
                part=np.argpartition(c_seg, -kmax)[-kmax:]
                sel=part
             mask=np.zeros(L, dtype=bool); mask[sel]=True; mask[M]=True
             if not mask.all():
                skipp=c_seg[~mask]
                if skipp.size:
                   unaff=max(unaff, float(skipp.max()) + 1.01)
             rels=np.nonzero(mask)[0]
          # build slopes/intercepts
          center_rel=M
          noncenters=[]; center_val_c=None
          for r in rels:
             d = r - M
             a=0.0
             if d >=0:
                if idx-d >=0: a += 1.0 - hs[idx-d]
                if idx+d < n: a -= hs[idx+d]
             else:
                e=-d
                if idx+e < n: a += 1.0 - hs[idx+e]
                if idx-e >=0: a -= hs[idx-e]
             if r == center_rel:
                center_val_c = float(c_seg[r])
             else:
                noncenters.append((a, float(c_seg[r]) - a*h0))
          if not noncenters:
             slopes=np.array([]); intercepts=np.array([])
          else:
             slopes=np.array([t[0] for t in noncenters]); intercepts=np.array([t[1] for t in noncenters])
          bx,bv=minimize_h_axis(slopes,intercepts,center_val_c,h0,unaff)
          if bv < current_max - 1e-8 and abs(bx-h0)>1e-12:
             h_new[idx]=bx; improved=True
       # after all coords
       if improved:
          h=project_feasible(h_new,n)
          nc=compute_c5(h,n)
          if nc < best_c - 1e-14:
             best_c=nc; no_improve=0
          else:
             no_improve+=1
          if no_improve > 3 and (p%30==0):
             # maybe still continue; no break to allow? If no c improvement 3 passes, break
             if no_improve > 6: break
       else:
          # project maybe to fix sum? if h already feasible
          break
    return h,best_c
```
Need current_max raw; best_c includes dx? `compute_c5` returns scaled. Compare best_c scaled; bv raw. For update predicted, compare raw current_max. For best_c updates, compute scaled. Fine.

Starting points:
```
def make_sinusoidal(n, freq=1.0, phase=0.0):
    x = np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    h = 0.5 + 0.5*np.sin(np.pi*freq*x + phase)
    return project_feasible(h,n)

def make_block_periodic(n, period, ones):
    h = np.zeros(n)
    for i in range(n):
       if (i % period) in ones: h[i]=1.0
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
Need no large lists; ones list small. Or use `mask = np.arange(n) % period; h = np.isin(mask, ...).astype(float)`. `np.isin` maybe okay.

Maybe make a "two-value sawtooth": h = ((x/q) %1 < r). But simple.

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
  t0=time.time()
  deadline=t0+budget_s-10
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  best_h=None; best_c=1.0; best_n=0
  try: h_init=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy(); n0=len(h_init)
  except: h_init=None; n0=600
  if h_init is None: h_init=make_sinusoidal(600,1.0,0.0); n0=600
  process h: project, eval; if c<best set.
  if best_c > 0.3808:
      # coordinate initial
      h1,c1=coord_cd(h_init,n0,60,deadline-250,k_all=1200); update
      # LSE
      h2,c2=lse_optimize(h1,n0,alphas=[30,100,300],iters_per=80,deadline=deadline-150); update
      # coord final
      h3,c3=coord_cd(h2,n0,80,deadline-60,k_all=1200); update
  # try alternatives if time > 120
  if time.time() < deadline - 120:
      x_cur = np.linspace(0,2,len(best_h))
      for n2 in [200,300,400,500]: # maybe if best_n 600, smaller
         if time > deadline-60 break
         # start from best interpolated
         h_int = project_feasible(np.interp(np.linspace(0,2,n2), x_cur, best_h), n2)
         hi,ci=coord_cd(h_int,n2,30,deadline-40,k_all=1200)
         if ci < best_c: best_h=hi; best_c=ci; best_n=n2; x_cur=...
      # alternative starts
      for n2 in [240,360,480]:
         for freq in [1.0,1.5,2.0]:
          h_alt=make_sinusoidal(n2,freq,0.0)
          if time... coord_cd 15 passes, update
  # final quick coord on best
  if time < deadline-10: h,c=coord_cd(best_h,best_n,20,deadline-5,k_all=1200); update
  best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n); c=compute_c5
  return best_h,c,best_n
```

So `coord_cd` phase:
```
h_cur=best_h.copy(); best_c=...
for pass:
  c=...; hs=h_cur; h_new=...
  improved=False
  ... update h_new if pred improves current raw max
  if not improved: break
  best_pass_h=None
  # candidates: full, mixtures
  alphas = [1.0,0.75,0.5,0.25,0.1]
  # We can skip 1 if projection of full worse? evaluate all choose best < best_c
  best_trial=None
  for a in alphas:
    if a>0.999: cand=h_new
    else: cand=(1-a)*h_cur + a*h_new
    cand=project_feasible(cand,n)
    nc=compute_c5(cand,n)
    if nc < best_c - 1e-13 and (best_trial is None or nc < best_trial_c): best_trial=cand,...
  if best_trial: best_h=best_trial; best_c=...; h_cur=best_h; stall=0
  else: stall++ ; if stall>5 break
```

```
def enforce_sum(h,n):
    target=n*0.5
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
        idxs=np.where((h>1e-15)&(h<1-1e-15))[0]
        if len(idxs)>0:
            # sort by distance from target? choose one with enough room
            if diff>0:
               i=idxs[np.argmax(1-h[idxs])]
            else:
               i=idxs[np.argmax(h[idxs])]
            h[i]+=diff
            h=np.clip(h,0,1)
        # if still diff (due bound), adjust among all with small scaling
        diff2=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff2)>1e-15:
            if diff2>0:
               room=1-h; tot=room.sum(); if tot>0: h += diff2/tot*room
            else:
               tot=h.sum(); if tot>0: h += diff2/tot*h
            h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```

Let's refine `coord_cd` with trial mixtures:
```
def coord_cd(h,n,max_passes,deadline,k_all=2000):
    best=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    best_c=compute_c5(best,n)
    center=n-1
    stall=0
    for p in range(max_passes):
        if time.time()>deadline: break
        cur=best
        c_full=np.correlate(cur,1.0-cur,mode='full')
        current_max=float(c_full.max())
        cur_copy=cur
        h_new=cur.copy()
        improved=False
        for idx in range(n):
            if (idx & 31)==0 and time.time()>deadline: break
            h0=cur_copy[idx]
            if h0 <=1e-13 or h0 >=1.0-1e-13: continue
            other=M? Use h0 from cur_copy.
            M=idx if idx>n-1-idx else n-1-idx
            p_lo=center-M; L=2*M+1
            c_seg=c_full[p_lo:p_lo+L]
            unaff=0.0
            if p_lo>0: unaff=max(unaff,float(c_full[:p_lo].max()))
            p_hi=p_lo+L-1
            if p_hi<2*n-2: unaff=max(unaff,float(c_full[p_hi+1:].max()))
            if L <= k_all:
                rels=np.arange(L)
                # no skip
            else:
                kmax=min(800, k_all)
                thresh=float(c_seg.max())-1.5
                sel=np.flatnonzero(c_seg >= thresh)
                if sel.size>kmax:
                    sel=np.argpartition(c_seg, -kmax)[-kmax:]
                mask=np.zeros(L,bool); mask[sel]=True; mask[M]=True
                if not mask.all():
                    skipl=c_seg[~mask]
                    if skipl.size: unaff=max(unaff,float(skipl.max())+1.01)
                rels=np.nonzero(mask)[0]
            # build lists
            slopes_list=[]; inter_list=[]; center_c=None
            for r in rels:
                d=r-M
                a=0.0
                if d>=0:
                    if idx-d>=0: a += 1.0 - cur_copy[idx-d]
                    if idx+d<n: a -= cur_copy[idx+d]
                else:
                    e=-d
                    if idx+e<n: a += 1.0 - cur_copy[idx+e]
                    if idx-e>=0: a -= cur_copy[idx-e]
                if r==M:
                    center_c=float(c_seg[r])
                else:
                    slopes_list.append(a); inter_list.append(float(c_seg[r]) - a*h0)
            slopes=np.array(slopes_list) if slopes_list else np.empty(0)
            intercepts=np.array(inter_list) if inter_list else np.empty(0)
            bx,bv=minimize_h_axis(slopes,intercepts,center_c,h0,unaff)
            if abs(bx-h0)>1e-12 and bv < current_max - 1e-8:
                h_new[idx]=bx; improved=True
        if not improved:
            break
        # line search mixtures
        best_trial=None; best_trial_c=best_c
        # use alphas [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.75]
        for mix in (1.0,0.6,0.35,0.15):
            if time.time()>deadline: break
            if mix > 0.999:
                cand=h_new
            else:
                cand=(1.0-mix)*cur + mix*h_new
            cand=project_feasible(cand,n)
            cc=compute_c5(cand,n)
            if cc < best_trial_c - 1e-14:
                best_trial_c=cc; best_trial=cand
        if best_trial is not None:
            best=best_trial; best_c=best_trial_c; stall=0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 5: break
    return project_feasible(best,n), best_c
```
Note if `best_trial_c` initialized best_c; if mixtures worsen, none. If h_new has improvements but projection worsens, no accept. Could still accept a small mix that is not globally lower but lower than current? It must be lower. Good.

Now LSE optimize. To reduce project cost, we can use `project_feasible` with water fill. Code:
```
def lse_optimize(h,n,max_iters_each,alphas,deadline):
    best=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=compute_c5(best,n); dx=2.0/n
    for alpha in alphas:
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      cur=best; cur_c=best_c
      for it in range(max_iters_each):
        if time.time()>deadline: break
        c=np.correlate(cur,1-cur,mode='full')
        cmax=float(c.max())
        w=np.exp(alpha*(c-cmax))
        wsum=float(w.sum())
        if wsum <=0: break
        w /= wsum
        wp=w[n-1:2*n-1]
        wn=np.zeros(n)
        if n>1: wn[1:]=w[n-2::-1]
        K=wp+wn; K[0]=2*wp[0]
        Kp=np.zeros(2*n-1)
        Kp[n-1]=K[0]
        if n>1:
          Kp[:n-1]=K[1:][::-1]
          Kp[n:]=K[1:]
        need=3*n-2
        nfft=1
        while nfft<need: nfft*=2
        th=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(cur,nfft)*np.fft.rfft(Kp,nfft),nfft)[n-1:n-1+n]
        cp=np.cumsum(wp)
        pn=np.cumsum(wn)
        tn=wn.sum()-pn
        g=dx*(cp+tn-th)
        g-=g.mean()
        gn=float(np.sqrt(np.dot(g,g)))
        if gn <1e-15: break
        base=0.2/gn
        accepted=False
        for mult in (0.1,0.3,0.6,1.2,0.05,0.02):
           trial=project_feasible(cur - base*mult*g,n)
           tc=compute_c5(trial,n)
           if tc < best_c - 1e-13:
               best=trial; best_c=tc; cur=trial; accepted=True; break
        if not accepted:
           break
    return best,best_c
```
Maybe if alpha small, step not enough; if no accepted, try negative? no.

Could also accept local `cur_c` if tc<cur_c even if not best? But if not best, maybe later; accept to continue. If tc not < best but < cur, could lead to best. Use `if tc < best_c`? Since best_c tracks global. If cur=best always if accepted; so same. If a trial worsens best, not accept. Good.

We can run LSE with alphas [20,60,200] each 50. Could be enough.

```
def enforce_sum(h,n):
  target=0.5*n
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  diff=target-h.sum()
  for _ in range(3):
    if abs(diff)<=1e-14: break
    interior=np.where((h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12))[0]
    if interior.size:
       if diff>0: i=interior[int(np.argmax(1.0-h[interior]))]
       else: i=interior[int(np.argmax(h[interior]))]
       add=diff
       if 0 <= h[i]+add <=1:
          h[i]+=add
       else:
          # distribute
          if diff>0:
             room=1-h; tot=room.sum(); h += (diff/tot)*room if tot>0 else 0
          else:
             tot=h.sum(); h += (diff/tot)*h if tot>0 else 0
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       diff=target-h.sum()
    else:
       # try scale non-saturated? none
       h=np.clip(h + diff/n,0,1)
       diff=target-h.sum()
  return h
```
Good.

def make_sinusoidal(n, freq=1.0, phase=0.0):

Detailed code snippets:

`enforce_sum`:
```
def enforce_sum(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(4):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-14:
            break
        interior = np.where((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12))[0]
        if interior.size > 0:
            if diff > 0:
                i = interior[int(np.argmax(1.0 - h[interior]))]
            else:
                i = interior[int(np.argmax(h[interior]))]
            if 0.0 <= h[i] + diff <= 1.0:
                h[i] += diff
            else:
                if diff > 0:
                    room = 1.0 - h
                    tot = float(room.sum())
                    if tot > 0:
                        h += (diff / tot) * room
                else:
                    tot = float(h.sum())
                    if tot > 0:
                        h += (diff / tot) * h
                h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            h = np.clip(h + diff / max(1, n), 0.0, 1.0)
    return h
```
If `h + diff/n` with n int ok.

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    target=0.5*n
    if h.size==0: return h
    if float(h.min()) >= -1e-12 and float(h.max()) <= 1+1e-12:
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       if abs(target-float(h.sum())) <= 1e-10:
          return enforce_sum(h,n)
    lo = -float(np.max(h)) - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(h)) + 1.0
    if hi < lo: lo,hi=hi,lo
    for _ in range(50):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=float(np.sum(np.clip(h+mid,0,1)))
       if s > target: hi=mid
       else: lo=mid
    h=np.clip(h+0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    return enforce_sum(h,n)
```
If hi<lo due? no.

`compute_c5`:
```
def compute_c5(h,n):
    dx=2.0/float(n)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full'))) * dx
```
If h empty? no.

`build_hull` careful with `skip`:
```
def build_hull(m, b):
    order=np.argsort(m, kind='mergesort')
    ms=m[order]; bs=b[order]
    hm=[]; hb=[]; hx=[]
    eps=1e-14
    for i in range(len(ms)):
       mv=float(ms[i]); bv=float(bs[i])
       skip=False
       while len(hm) and abs(mv-hm[-1]) < eps:
           if bv <= hb[-1] + 1e-15:
               skip=True
               break
           hm.pop(); hb.pop(); hx.pop()
       if skip: continue
       if not len(hm):
           hm.append(mv); hb.append(bv); hx.append(-np.inf); continue
       denom=mv-hm[-1]
       if abs(denom) < eps:
           if bv > hb[-1]: hb[-1]=bv
           continue
       x=(hb[-1]-bv)/denom
       while len(hm) and x <= hx[-1] + 1e-12:
           hm.pop(); hb.pop(); hx.pop()
           if not len(hm): break
           # if same slope as previous after pop
           if abs(mv-hm[-1]) < eps:
               if bv <= hb[-1] + 1e-15:
                   # new line not better than previous; but we are in while, should skip add?
                   skip=True
                   break
               else:
                   hm.pop(); hb.pop(); hx.pop()
                   if not len(hm): break
                   continue
           denom=mv-hm[-1]
           if abs(denom) < eps:
               if bv > hb[-1]:
                   hb[-1]=bv
               x = hx[-1] + 1.0 # large, but better avoid
               continue
           x=(hb[-1]-bv)/denom
       if skip: continue
       if not len(hm):
           hm.append(mv); hb.append(bv); hx.append(-np.inf)
       else:
           if not np.isfinite(x):
               x = 1e300 if x > 0 else -1e300
           hm.append(mv); hb.append(bv); hx.append(float(x))
    return hm,hb,hx
```
This is messy. Since duplicates rare, simplify: before loop, combine equal slopes using dictionary? Better: sort and process unique slopes:
```
unique_m=[]; unique_b=[]
last_m=None
for mvals,bvals in zip(ms,bs):
  if last_m is None or abs(mvals-last_m)>1e-14:
     unique_m.append(mvals); unique_b.append(bvals); last_m=mvals
  else:
     if bvals > unique_b[-1]: unique_b[-1]=bvals
```
Then build hull with strictly increasing slopes. Simpler and robust. Use lists for unique. Then hull building no same slope handling except floating. Let's implement:
```
def build_hull(m,b):
  order=np.argsort(m)
  ms=m[order].astype(float); bs=b[order].astype(float)
  um=[]; ub=[]
  for i in range(len(ms)):
    if not um or ms[i]-um[-1] > 1e-14:
       um.append(float(ms[i])); ub.append(float(bs[i]))
    else:
       if float(bs[i]) > ub[-1]: ub[-1]=float(bs[i])
  hm=[];hb=[];hx=[]
  for mv,bv in zip(um,ub):
    if not hm:
      hm.append(mv);hb.append(bv);hx.append(-np.inf)
    else:
      x=(hb[-1]-bv)/(mv-hm[-1])
      while len(hm) and x <= hx[-1] + 1e-12:
        hm.pop();hb.pop();hx.pop()
        if not hm: break
        x=(hb[-1]-bv)/(mv-hm[-1])
      if not hm:
        hm.append(mv);hb.append(bv);hx.append(-np.inf)
      else:
        hm.append(mv);hb.append(bv);hx.append(float(x))
  return hm,hb,hx
```
Good. Denom positive since slopes strictly increasing. If x nan? no.

`d_eval`.

`affine_min_x`:
```
def affine_min_x(hm,hx,h0):
    eps=1e-12
    if not hm: return 0.5
    if hm[0] > eps: return 0.0
    if hm[-1] < -eps: return 1.0
    for i in range(len(hm)-1):
        if hm[i] <= eps and hm[i+1] >= -eps:
            if abs(hm[i]) <= eps:
                a = 0.0 if i == 0 else float(hx[i])
                bnd = 1.0 if i+1 >= len(hx) else float(hx[i+1])
                if a < 0: a=0.0
                if bnd > 1: bnd=1.0
                if bnd < a: bnd, a = a, bnd
                if a <= h0 <= bnd: return h0
                return 0.5*(a+bnd)
            x = float(hx[i+1]) if i+1 < len(hx) else 1.0
            if x < 0.0: return 0.0
            if x > 1.0: return 1.0
            return x
    if hm[-1] <= eps: return 1.0
    return 0.0
```
If crossing at i where hm[i]<0 and hm[i+1]>0, `h0` not used; okay.

`minimize_h_axis`:
```
def minimize_h_axis(slopes, intercepts, center_c, h0, unaff):
    slopes = np.asarray(slopes, dtype=float)
    if slopes.size > 0:
        hm,hb,hx = build_hull(slopes, intercepts)
    else:
        hm,hb,hx = [],[],[]
    if center_c is None:
        Zc = None
    else:
        Zc = float(center_c) - float(h0) * (1.0 - float(h0))
    xA = affine_min_x(hm,hx,float(h0))
    cands = [float(h0), 0.0, 1.0, xA]
    if Zc is not None and hm:
        bestD = 1e300
        xD = float(h0)
        for i in range(len(hm)):
            a = -np.inf if i == 0 else float(hx[i])
            bnd = np.inf if i+1 >= len(hx) else float(hx[i+1])
            if a < 0.0: a = 0.0
            if bnd > 1.0: bnd = 1.0
            if bnd <= a:
                continue
            m = float(hm[i]); bb = float(hb[i])
            x0 = 0.5 * (1.0 - m)
            xi = x0
            if xi < a: xi = a
            elif xi > bnd: xi = bnd
            D = xi*xi + (m - 1.0)*xi + (bb - Zc)
            if D < bestD:
                bestD = D; xD = float(xi)
        cands.append(xD)
        if bestD < -1e-10:
            if d_eval(0.0, hm,hb,hx,Zc) < 0.0:
                left = 0.0
            else:
                a=0.0; b=xD
                # if d_eval(b) >=0 due numeric, no roots
                if d_eval(b, hm,hb,hx,Zc) < 0.0:
                    for _ in range(45):
                        mid=0.5*(a+b)
                        if d_eval(mid,hm,hb,hx,Zc) < 0.0: b=mid
                        else: a=mid
                    left=0.5*(a+b)
                else:
                    left=x D
            if d_eval(1.0, hm,hb,hx,Zc) < 0.0:
                right=1.0
            else:
                a=xD; b=1.0
                if d_eval(a,hm,hb,hx,Zc) < 0.0:
                    for _ in range(45):
                        mid=0.5*(a+b)
                        if d_eval(mid,hm,hb,hx,Zc) < 0.0: a=mid
                        else: b=mid
                    right=0.5*(a+b)
                else:
                    right=xD
            cands.append(left); cands.append(right)
    best_x = float(h0)
    best_val = 1e300
    for x in cands:
       x=float(x)
       if x < 0.0: x=0.0
       elif x > 1.0: x=1.0
       Av = active_value(x,hm,hb,hx)
       Hv = Av
       if Zc is not None:
          Zv = Zc + x - x*x
          if Zv > Hv: Hv=Zv
       if unaff > Hv: Hv=unaff
       if Hv < best_val:
          best_val=Hv; best_x=x
    return best_x, float(best_val)
```
In left if d_eval(b)>=0, set left=x D? But if bestD negative, d_eval(xD) should negative; numeric maybe. Use `left=x D` if not negative; okay.
Note `a=xD` in right could be -? xD clipped. Good.

`stall` if no accept; if improved but projection no accept, maybe try even smaller? We include. Stall 4 break.

In `coord_cd`, after a pass with accepted trial, `cur=best` next iteration. `h_new` not used. Good.

Now, `lse_optimize`: If n small, FFT need unique. Use `cur` as best. At each alpha, if no accepted after one? break alpha? It will break iterations and go next alpha. Maybe if alpha too small no accept, larger alpha might. Good.

Potential: `w[np.max]` with alpha large could underflow all but max to0; fine.

Now, final run. We'll structure:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+max(30.0,float(budget_s)-8.0)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h=None; best_c=1.0
    # init
    try:
       h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
       if h0.size==0: raise ValueError
       n0=int(h0.size)
    except Exception:
       n0=600
       h0=make_sinusoidal(n0,1.0,0.0)
    h0=project_feasible(h0,n0)
    c0=compute_c5(h0,n0)
    best_h=h0; best_c=c0
    # phase1 coord
    if time.time() < deadline - 200:
       h1,c1=coord_cd(best_h,len(best_h), max(20, int((deadline - time.time())*0.4)), deadline - 200, k_all=2000)
       if c1 < best_c: best=...
       else: # maybe still return even if not? coord_cd returns best from input? It maintains best; if no improvement returns original. c1>=best maybe. Update if <.
    # phase2 lse
    if time.time() < deadline - 90:
       h2,c2=lse_optimize(best_h,... alphas [20,80,300], 60, deadline-80)
       update
    # phase3 coord final
    if time.time() < deadline - 30:
       h3,c3=coord_cd(best_h,..., max(20,...), deadline-20, k_all=2000)
       update
    # transformations
    for arr in ...:
       cand=project_feasible(arr,n); cc=compute
       if cc<best_c: update
    # alternative if time and best_c > 0.3808 maybe
    if best_c > 0.3808 and time.time() < deadline - 100:
       n_best=len(best_h); x_best=np.linspace(0,2,n_best)
       for n_alt in (240,360,480):
          if time.time() > deadline - 40: break
          h_int=project_feasible(np.interp(np.linspace(0,2,n_alt),x_best,best_h),n_alt)
          ha,ca=coord_cd(h_int,n_alt,40,deadline-30,k_all=2000)
          if ca<best_c: best=...; x_best update? maybe.
       for n_alt in (160,240,320):
          for s in (1,2):
             if time... break
             h_r=make_random_continuous(n_alt, seed+s, 0.5)
             ha,ca=coord_cd(h_r,n_alt,30,deadline-20,k_all=2000)
             update
          for freq in (1.0,1.5,2.0):
             if time... break
             h_s=make_sinusoidal(n_alt,freq,0.0)
             ha,ca=coord_cd(h_s,n_alt,25,deadline-15,k_all=2000)
             update
    # final quick coord and transformation
    if time.time() < deadline - 5:
       hf,cf=coord_cd(best_h,len(best_h),20,deadline-2,k_all=2000); update
       arr transforms...
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,len(best_h))
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,len(best_h))
    return best_h,best_c,int(len(best_h))

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from bisect import bisect_right

def enforce_sum(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(4):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-14:
            break
        interior = np.where((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12))[0]
        if interior.size:
            if diff > 0.0:
                i = interior[int(np.argmax(1.0 - h[interior]))]
            else:
                i = interior[int(np.argmax(h[interior]))]
            if 0.0 <= h[i] + diff <= 1.0:
                h[i] += diff
            else:
                if diff > 0.0:
                    room = 1.0 - h
                    tot = float(room.sum())
                    if tot > 0.0:
                        h += (diff / tot) * room
                else:
                    tot = float(h.sum())
                    if tot > 0.0:
                        h += (diff / tot) * h
                h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            h = np.clip(h + diff / float(max(1, n)), 0.0, 1.0)
    return h

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = int(n)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    if h.size == 0:
        return h
    if float(h.min()) >= -1e-12 and float(h.max()) <= 1.0 + 1e-12:
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        if abs(target - float(h.sum())) <= 1e-10:
            return enforce_sum(h, n)
    lo = -float(np.max(h)) - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(h)) + 1.0
    if hi < lo:
        lo, hi = hi, lo
    for _ in range(50):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s > target:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid
    h = np.clip(h + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    return enforce_sum(h, n)

def compute_c5(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full"))) * dx

def build_hull(m, b):
    if m.size == 0:
        return [], [], []
    order = np.argsort(m, kind="mergesort")
    ms = m[order].astype(float)
    bs = b[order].astype(float)
    um, ub = [], []
    for i in range(len(ms)):
        if not um or ms[i] - um[-1] > 1e-14:
            um.append(float(ms[i]))
            ub.append(float(bs[i]))
        else:
            if float(bs[i]) > ub[-1]:
                ub[-1] = float(bs[i])
    hm, hb, hx = [], [], []
    for mv, bv in zip(um, ub):
        if not hm:
            hm.append(mv)
            hb.append(bv)
            hx.append(-np.inf)
            continue
        x = (hb[-1] - bv) / (mv - hm[-1])
        while len(hm) and x <= hx[-1] + 1e-12:
            hm.pop(); hb.pop(); hx.pop()
            if not hm:
                break
            x = (hb[-1] - bv) / (mv - hm[-1])
        if not hm:
            hm.append(mv); hb.append(bv); hx.append(-np.inf)
        else:
            if not np.isfinite(x):
                x = 1e300 if x > 0 else -1e300
            hm.append(mv); hb.append(bv); hx.append(float(x))
    return hm, hb, hx

def active_value(x, hm, hb, hx):
    if not hm:
        return -1e300
    i = bisect_right(hx, float(x)) - 1
    if i < 0:
        i = 0
    elif i >= len(hm):
        i = len(hm) - 1
    return hm[i] * float(x) + hb[i]

def d_eval(x, hm, hb, hx, Zc):
    x = float(x)
    i = bisect_right(hx, x) - 1
    if i < 0:
        i = 0
    elif i >= len(hm):
        i = len(hm) - 1
    A = hm[i] * x + hb[i]
    return A - (Zc + x - x * x)

def affine_min_x(hm, hx, h0):
    eps = 1e-12
    if not hm:
        return 0.5
    if hm[0] > eps:
        return 0.0
    if hm[-1] < -eps:
        return 1.0
    for i in range(len(hm) - 1):
        if hm[i] <= eps and hm[i + 1] >= -eps:
            if abs(hm[i]) <= eps:
                a = 0.0 if i == 0 else float(hx[i])
                b = 1.0 if i + 1 >= len(hx) else float(hx[i + 1])
                a = max(0.0, a)
                b = min(1.0, b)
                if a > b:
                    a, b = b, a
                if a <= h0 <= b:
                    return float(h0)
                return 0.5 * (a + b)
            x = float(hx[i + 1]) if i + 1 < len(hx) else 1.0
            if x < 0.0:
                return 0.0
            if x > 1.0:
                return 1.0
            return x
    return 1.0 if hm[-1] <= eps else 0.0

def minimize_h_axis(slopes, intercepts, center_c, h0, unaff):
    slopes = np.asarray(slopes, dtype=float)
    if slopes.size:
        hm, hb, hx = build_hull(slopes, intercepts)
    else:
        hm, hb, hx = [], [], []
    Zc = None if center_c is None else float(center_c) - float(h0) * (1.0 - float(h0))
    xA = affine_min_x(hm, hx, float(h0))
    cands = [float(h0), 0.0, 1.0, float(xA)]
    if Zc is not None and hm:
        bestD, xD = 1e300, float(h0)
        for i in range(len(hm)):
            a = -np.inf if i == 0 else float(hx[i])
            b = np.inf if i + 1 >= len(hx) else float(hx[i + 1])
            if a < 0.0:
                a = 0.0
            if b > 1.0:
                b = 1.0
            if b <= a:
                continue
            m = float(hm[i]); bb = float(hb[i])
            xi = 0.5 * (1.0 - m)
            if xi < a:
                xi = a
            elif xi > b:
                xi = b
            D = xi * xi + (m - 1.0) * xi + (bb - Zc)
            if D < bestD:
                bestD, xD = D, float(xi)
        cands.append(xD)
        if bestD < -1e-10:
            if d_eval(0.0, hm, hb, hx, Zc) < 0.0:
                left = 0.0
            else:
                a, b = 0.0, xD
                if d_eval(b, hm, hb, hx, Zc) < 0.0:
                    for _ in range(45):
                        mid = 0.5 * (a + b)
                        if d_eval(mid, hm, hb, hx, Zc) < 0.0:
                            b = mid
                        else:
                            a = mid
                    left = 0.5 * (a + b)
                else:
                    left = xD
            if d_eval(1.0, hm, hb, hx, Zc) < 0.0:
                right = 1.0
            else:
                a, b = xD, 1.0
                if d_eval(a, hm, hb, hx, Zc) < 0.0:
                    for _ in range(45):
                        mid = 0.5 * (a + b)
                        if d_eval(mid, hm, hb, hx, Zc) < 0.0:
                            a = mid
                        else:
                            b = mid
                    right = 0.5 * (a + b)
                else:
                    right = xD
            cands.append(left)
            cands.append(right)
    best_x = float(h0)
    best_val = 1e300
    for x in cands:
        x = float(x)
        if x < 0.0:
            x = 0.0
        elif x > 1.0:
            x = 1.0
        Av = active_value(x, hm, hb, hx)
        Hv = Av
        if Zc is not None:
            Zv = Zc + x - x * x
            if Zv > Hv:
                Hv = Zv
        if unaff > Hv:
            Hv = float(unaff)
        if Hv < best_val:
            best_val, best_x = Hv, x
    return best_x, float(best_val)

def coord_cd(h, n, max_passes, deadline, k_all=2000):
    best = project_feasible(h, n).copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best, n)
    center = int(n) - 1
    full_len = 2 * int(n) - 1
    stall = 0
    max_passes = int(max_passes)
    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        cur = best
        c_full = np.correlate(cur, 1.0 - cur, mode="full")
        pref = np.empty(full_len + 1, dtype=float)
        pref[0] = 0.0
        np.maximum.accumulate(c_full, out=pref[1:])
        suff = np.empty(full_len + 1, dtype=float)
        suff[full_len] = 0.0
        suff[:full_len] = np.maximum.accumulate(c_full[::-1])[::-1]
        current_max = float(pref[full_len])
        h_new = cur.copy()
        improved = False
        for idx in range(n):
            if (idx & 31) == 0 and time.time() > deadline:
                break
            h0 = cur[idx]
            if h0 <= 1e-13 or h0 >= 1.0 - 1e-13:
                continue
            M = idx if idx > n - 1 - idx else n - 1 - idx
            p_lo = center - M
            L = 2 * M + 1
            p_hi = p_lo + L - 1
            c_seg = c_full[p_lo:p_lo + L]
            unaff = float(max(pref[p_lo], suff[p_hi + 1]))
            if L <= int(k_all):
                slopes = np.empty(max(0, L - 1), dtype=float)
                intercepts = np.empty(max(0, L - 1), dtype=float)
                center_c = None
                cnt = 0
                for r in range(L):
                    d = r - M
                    a = 0.0
                    if d >= 0:
                        if idx - d >= 0:
                            a += 1.0 - cur[idx - d]
                        if idx + d < n:
                            a -= cur[idx + d]
                    else:
                        e = -d
                        if idx + e < n:
                            a += 1.0 - cur[idx + e]
                        if idx - e >= 0:
                            a -= cur[idx - e]
                    if r == M:
                        center_c = float(c_seg[r])
                    else:
                        slopes[cnt] = a
                        intercepts[cnt] = float(c_seg[r]) - a * h0
                        cnt += 1
                if cnt < slopes.size:
                    slopes = slopes[:cnt]
                    intercepts = intercepts[:cnt]
            else:
                kmax = min(int(k_all), L)
                sel = np.flatnonzero(c_seg >= (current_max - 1.5))
                if sel.size > kmax:
                    sel = np.argpartition(c_seg, -kmax)[-kmax:]
                mask = np.zeros(L, dtype=bool)
                mask[sel] = True
                mask[M] = True
                if not mask.all():
                    sk = c_seg[~mask]
                    if sk.size:
                        unaff = max(unaff, float(sk.max()) + 1.01)
                rels = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
                sl, ib, center_c = [], [], None
                for r in rels:
                    d = r - M
                    a = 0.0
                    if d >= 0:
                        if idx - d >= 0:
                            a += 1.0 - cur[idx - d]
                        if idx + d < n:
                            a -= cur[idx + d]
                    else:
                        e = -d
                        if idx + e < n:
                            a += 1.0 - cur[idx + e]
                        if idx - e >= 0:
                            a -= cur[idx - e]
                    if r == M:
                        center_c = float(c_seg[r])
                    else:
                        sl.append(a)
                        ib.append(float(c_seg[r]) - a * h0)
                slopes = np.asarray(sl, dtype=float)
                intercepts = np.asarray(ib, dtype=float)
            bx, bv = minimize_h_axis(slopes, intercepts, center_c, h0, unaff)
            if abs(bx - h0) > 1e-12 and bv < current_max - 1e-8:
                h_new[idx] = bx
                improved = True
        if not improved:
            break
        trial_best = None
        trial_c = best_c
        for mix in (1.0, 0.6, 0.3, 0.15):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            cand = h_new if mix > 0.999 else (1.0 - mix) * cur + mix * h_new
            cand = project_feasible(cand, n)
            cc = compute_c5(cand, n)
            if cc < trial_c - 1e-14:
                trial_c = cc
                trial_best = cand
        if trial_best is not None:
            best = trial_best
            best_c = trial_c
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 5:
                break
    return project_feasible(best, n), best_c

def lse_optimize(h, n, alphas, max_iters_each, deadline):
    best = project_feasible(h, n).copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best, n)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        cur, cur_c = best, best_c
        for _ in range(int(max_iters_each)):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            c = np.correlate(cur, 1.0 - cur, mode="full")
            cmax = float(c.max())
            w = np.exp(float(alpha) * (c - cmax))
            wsum = float(w.sum())
            if wsum <= 0.0:
                break
            w /= wsum
            wp = w[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
            wn = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
            if n > 1:
                wn[1:] = w[n - 2::-1]
            K = wp + wn
            K[0] = 2.0 * wp[0]
            Kp = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
            Kp[n - 1] = K[0]
            if n > 1:
                Kp[:n - 1] = K[1:][::-1]
                Kp[n:] = K[1:]
            need = 3 * n - 2
            nfft = 1
            while nfft < need:
                nfft <<= 1
            th = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(cur, nfft) * np.fft.rfft(Kp, nfft), nfft)[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
            g = dx * (np.cumsum(wp) + (float(wn.sum()) - np.cumsum(wn)) - th)
            g -= float(g.mean())
            gn = float(np.sqrt(np.dot(g, g)))
            if gn < 1e-15:
                break
            base = 0.2 / gn
            accepted = False
            for mult in (0.1, 0.3, 0.6, 1.2, 0.05, 0.02):
                trial = project_feasible(cur - base * mult * g, n)
                tc = compute_c5(trial, n)
                if tc < best_c - 1e-13:
                    best = trial
                    best_c = tc
                    cur = trial
                    cur_c = tc
                    accepted = True
                    break
            if not accepted:
                break
    return best, best_c

def make_sinusoidal(n, freq=1.0, phase=0.0):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, int(n))
    h = 0.5 + 0.45 * np.sin(np.pi * float(freq) * x + float(phase))
    return project_feasible(h, n)

def make_random_continuous(n, seed, width=0.5):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    h = 0.5 + float(width) * (rng.rand(int(n)) - 0.5)
    return project_feasible(h, n)

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(30.0, float(budget_s) - 8.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    try:
        h0 = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
        if h0.size == 0:
            raise ValueError
        n0 = int(h0.size)
    except Exception:
        n0 = 600
        h0 = make_sinusoidal(n0, 1.0, 0.0)
    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    best_h = h0
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n0)

    if time.time() < deadline - 180.0:
        h1, c1 = coord_cd(best_h, len(best_h), max(30, int(0.45 * (deadline - time.time()))), deadline - 180.0, 2000)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h1, c1

    if time.time() < deadline - 90.0:
        h2, c2 = lse_optimize(best_h, len(best_h), (20.0, 80.0, 300.0), 70, deadline - 80.0)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h2, c2

    if time.time() < deadline - 35.0:
        h3, c3 = coord_cd(best_h, len(best_h), max(25, int(0.4 * (deadline - time.time() - 5.0))), deadline - 25.0, 2000)
        if c3 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h3, c3

    nb = int(len(best_h))
    for arr in (1.0 - best_h, best_h[::-1], 1.0 - best_h[::-1]):
        cand = project_feasible(arr, nb)
        cc = compute_c5(cand, nb)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = cand, cc

    if best_c > 0.3808 and time.time() < deadline - 90.0:
        xb = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(best_h))
        for n_alt in (240, 360, 480):
            if time.time() > deadline - 35.0:
                break
            xi = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_alt)
            h_int = project_feasible(np.interp(xi, xb, best_h), n_alt)
            ha, ca = coord_cd(h_int, n_alt, 35, deadline - 25.0, 2000)
            if ca < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = ha, ca
                xb = xi
        for n_alt in (160, 240, 320):
            for s in (1, 2, int(seed) + 3):
                if time.time() > deadline - 20.0:
                    break
                h_r = make_random_continuous(n_alt, s, 0.5)
                ha, ca = coord_cd(h_r, n_alt, 25, deadline - 12.0, 2000)
                if ca < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c = ha, ca
            for fr in (1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
                if time.time() > deadline - 15.0:
                    break
                h_s = make_sinusoidal(n_alt, fr, 0.0)
                ha, ca = coord_cd(h_s, n_alt, 22, deadline - 10.0, 2000)
                if ca < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c = ha, ca

    if time.time() < deadline - 6.0:
        hf, cf = coord_cd(best_h, len(best_h), 18, deadline - 3.0, 2000)
        if cf < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hf, cf
        nb = int(len(best_h))
        for arr in (1.0 - best_h, best_h[::-1], 1.0 - best_h[::-1]):
            cand = project_feasible(arr, nb)
            cc = compute_c5(cand, nb)
            if cc < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = cand, cc

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, len(best_h))
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(len(best_h))
```
